## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos, and I'll tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 해볼 수 있는 일로는, 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 찾아 고치는 일이 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕히 가세요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは速さを優先してそのまま送る通信です。

TCPは届いたか確認して再送し、順番も揃えて届けます。UDPは確認も再送もせず、届かないことや順番が前後することがあります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar o limpiar tu rama local que aún no has compartido, dejando el historial lineal. Usa merge para integrar ramas que otros ya usan o cuando quieres conservar el historial tal cual ocurrió, y nunca hagas rebase de commits ya publicados.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的：收发前先建立连接，数据有确认、丢了重发、按顺序交付；UDP 则是无连接、不保证送达的，发了就不管，换来更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed work to tidy it into a clean, linear history before you share it. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rewriting it would break other people's copies.
```

bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

路径可以写全也可以只写一部分，Bun 会按路径里的文字匹配文件名。要只跑某个用例，再加 `-t`：

```bash
bun test foo -t "某个用例的名字"
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change is written down before it is applied to the actual data, so that if the system crashes midway, it can replay the log and recover without losing committed work. It trades a small amount of extra write work for durability and fast, safe recovery.
```

  [an app card under the message: made "京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1992 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五京沪高铁 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="top"></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="topsub"></div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h" id="day"></h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记</div>
</div>
<script>
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
function render(){
  var days = ducter.get('fares') || [];
  var latest = days[days.length-1];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  var top = document.getElementById('top');
  var sub = document.getElementById('topsub');
  var day = document.getElementById('day');
  if(!latest || !latest.trains || !latest.trains.length){
    top.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    sub.textContent = '每天早上看一次，600 以下的会列在这里';
    day.textContent = '';
    list.innerHTML = '';
    empty.hidden = false;
    return;
  }
  empty.hidden = true;
  day.textContent = latest.day + ' · ' + latest.date;
  var under = latest.trains.filter(function(t){return t.price < 600;}).sort(function(a,b){return a.price-b.price;});
  if(under.length){
    top.textContent = '¥' + under[0].price;
    sub.textContent = under.length + ' 班低于 600 元：' + under.map(function(t){return t.no;}).join('、');
  } else {
    top.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">无</span>';
    sub.textContent = '今天没有 600 元以下的班次';
  }
  list.innerHTML = latest.trains.map(function(t){
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.no) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(t.seats || '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div></div>';
  }).join('');
  if(window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(under.length ? under.length + ' 班低于 600，最低 ¥' + under[0].price : '今天没有 600 以下的');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fares":[{"day":"第 3 天","date":"11-25","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":""}]}]}

  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">Each night's write benchmark appears here: writes/s, p99 latency and compaction stalls.</div>

<style>
  .k-app{font-family:-apple-system,system-ui,sans-serif;color:var(--d-ink);background:var(--d-bg);padding:16px;max-width:360px;margin:0 auto}
  .k-sum{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:14px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:16px}
  .k-sum-label{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted)}
  .k-sum-value{font-size:32px;font-weight:600;margin:4px 0}
  .k-sum-sub{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-soft)}
  .k-none{color:var(--d-muted)}
  .k-h{font-size:15px;font-weight:600;margin:0 0 8px}
  .k-card{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:14px;padding:8px 12px}

  .k-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;al … uted);padding:12px 0;text-align:center}
</style>
<script>
  function fmt(n){ return n.toLocaleString('en-US'); }
  function render(runs){
    runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    if (!runs.length){ list.innerHTML=''; empty.style.display='block'; return; }
    empty.style.display='none';
    var latest = runs[runs.length-1];
    document.getElementById('w').textContent = fmt(latest.writes) + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'p99 ' + latest.p99 + ' ms · ' + latest.date;
    list.innerHTML = runs.slice().reverse().map(function(r){
      var stalls = (r.stalls === null || r.stalls === undefined) ? '—' : r.stalls + ' stalls';
      return '<div class="k-row"><div><div class="k-row-main">Night ' + r.night + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + r.date + ' · ' + stalls + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(r.writes) + '/s<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms</div></div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function load(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(render); }
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>

})();

function yuan(n){return "¥"+Number(n||0).toLocaleString("en-US");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];

  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?yuan(sum):"¥0";
  document.getElementById("total").classList.toggle("k-none",!xs.length);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?"共 "+xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=yuan(x.yuan);
    row.append(main,sub,val);
    ul.appendChild(row);
  }
}
ducter.summary("共 "+((ducter.get("expenses")||[]).length)+" 笔");
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();

    var noteEl = document.getElementById('note');
    var amt = parseFloat(amtEl.value);
    if (!(amt > 0)) { amtEl.focus(); return; }

    amtEl.value = '';
    noteEl.value = '';
    amtEl.focus();
    save();

    var row = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
    if (!row) return;
    var id = Number(row.getAttribute('data-id'));
    var item = items.filter(function (i) { return i.id === id; })[0];
    if (!item) return;
    Promise.resolve(ducter.ask('Delete this spend of ' + money(item.amt) + '?')).then(function (r) {
      var answer = String(r || '').toLowerCase();
      if (!r || answer === 'no' || answer === 'hold') return;
      items = items.filter(function (i) { return i.id !== id; });
      save();
    });
  });

  load();
})();

  .k-row { align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
  .k-row input[type=checkbox] { width: 20px; height: 20px; flex: 0 0 auto; accent-color: var(--d-accent); }

<script>
  const PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];

  function render() {

    PRS.forEach(p => {
      const isDone = !!landed[p.id];
      const li = document.createElement('li');
      li.className = 'k-row' + (isDone ? ' is-done' : '');
      const cb = document.createElement('input');
      cb.type = 'checkbox';
      cb.checked = isDone;
      cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
        landed[p.id] = cb.checked;
        Promise.resolve(ducter.set('landed', landed)).then(render);
        render();
      });
      const main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.innerHTML = '<div>' + p.repo + ' ' + p.num + ' · ' + p.title + '</div>' +
                       '<div class="k-row-sub">' + p.waiting + '</div>';
      li.appendChild(cb);
      li.appendChild(main);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    const open = PRS.filter(p => !landed[p.id]).length;
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = open;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open === 0 ? 'All landed' : 'Tick each one off as it lands';
    ducter.summary(open + ' open');
  }

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); });
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter","num":"#412","title":"Compaction cut","waiting":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem","num":"#10287","title":"Retry on 502","waiting":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb","num":"#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","waiting":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter","num":"#418","title":"Apps bar","waiting":"Draft, not sent for review yet"}]}
  [also used tools] app

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让修测试的那个任务再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期的那一刻，补完告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

要做的：在 TTL 测试里再加一条，覆盖缓存项恰好到期的那一刻（时间推进到 TTL 整点时的行为）。先看代码里的到期判断是包含还是不包含边界，按现有语义写断言，并在测试名里说明。继续用假时钟，不用真实计时。

完成标准：新测试通过；把测试输出（至少包括这条新测试和全部测试的结果）和连续运行的结果一起报告。

不做的事：不合并、不推送、不开 PR，不新开分支，留在现在的分支上。
```
